# Summary

I am MacBeth, a category theorist working on containers, directed containers, and polynomial
functors, for the Kodamai AI Mathematician grant (PI: Neil Ghani; my human: Robin Langer).

**This file is current-state synthesis, organized by FRONT — not history.**
- Chronology → `dream-journal/YYYY-MM-DD.md` (one per cycle).
- Proof grades (proved / computed / lean-verified / in-progress) → `proofs/registry/*.json` — **the registry wins any disagreement with this file.**
- Crown claims + their citations → the auto-memory index `MEMORY.md` (`[[slug]]` links below point there).
- Source provenance (arXiv IDs, deep-read vs agent-summary) → `reading/sources.json` (validated by `trustcheck.py`).

*Compressed 2026-08-30 from ~1690 chronological lines. Nothing proved was demoted; the dated
session blocks now live only in `dream-journal/`. Two external citations that had no home outside
the old SUMMARY are preserved in the ledger below: Katharopoulos linear-attention `2006.16236`,
Kock "Polynomial functors and trees" `0807.2874`.*

The predecessor's "p.r.a. ⟺ left adjoint" was **base-specific**; the responsible feature is that **`Set` has no zero object**, and the exact measure of the gap is the object `1◁q`.

- **★★ ADMISSIBILITY + the CONNECTEDNESS CONVERSE — DONE, `proved` (2026-08-30, THIRD PROVE of the day; closes Gap 3 of the bullet above).** Registry `left-adjoint-over-vec` subtree `gap3-converse`; proof `proofs/2026-08-30-admissibility-and-the-connectedness-converse.md`; code `scratch/connectedness-converse/verify.py`; collaborator note `for-collaborator/2026-08-30-admissibility-and-connectedness-converse.md`. **Question: is CONNECTEDNESS of `I` NECESSARY for `(−)◁q` to have a left adjoint? Four results.**
  **(A) The §9bis dichotomy is REFUTED by `Set_*`.** Pointed sets under smash have a **zero object** (`1_C ≅ 0_C = ∗`), so the copower criterion is vacuous — yet `Fam(Set_*^op)` is **not** closed under `◁`: for `p=⟨S^0∨S^0⟩`, `q=({1,2},(S^0,S^0))`, `⟦p⟧⟦q⟧X=(X∨X)^2`, and matching `|⋁_d[N_d,X]| = 1+Σ_d((m+1)^{n_d}−1)` forces `b=4` summands with `n_d=2` and `a=−4` with `n_d=1`. **A zero object does NOT buy admissibility.** `Set_*` is on **neither** pole: not extensive (wedge identifies basepoints; `γ` fails by NON-injectivity), not additive, not collapse (`S^0` tiny but `S^0∨S^0` not). It is the base where both known mechanisms switch off.
  **(B) THEOREM B — on the extensive pole, admissibility FORCES connectedness.** `C` nontrivial, **infinitary lextensive + cartesian closed**, `Fam(C^op)` closed under `◁` ⟹ `1 = I` is **connected**, hence (Thm 1) `(−)◁q` has a left adjoint for **every** `q`. Mechanism: `1` disconnected ⟹ `1 ≅ A⊔B` nontrivially ⟹ `C ≃ C/A × C/B`, and `[A,T·1] = (T·1_1, 1_2)` while copowers of `1` are `(E·1_1,E·1_2)` with **ONE external `E`** — forcing `E=1` then `|T|=1`. Ingredient: **Lemma E1** (lextensive, `1 ≅ 1⊔Z` ⟹ `Z ≅ 0`; `θι_1` is a map to the terminal hence `id`, then disjointness vs. pullback-along-identity). **So on that whole pole a counterexample to Thm 1's converse CANNOT BE POSED**, and `I` connected ⟺ `◁` exists ⟺ left adjoint for every `q`. Subsumes and relocates Prop 9.1 (`Set×Set`): visible already at `p=⟨A⟩` for `A` any nontrivial summand of `1`.
  **(C) LEMMA D — on the collapse pole the unit is ALWAYS disconnected.** Every object copower-tiny (the T4-left Prop 2.1 hypothesis) ⟹ `I` disconnected, by a diagram chase with **no cardinality argument**: connectedness would make `(d,f)↦ι_d f` a bijection `C(I⊔I,I)⊔C(I⊔I,I) → C(I⊔I,I⊔I)`, giving `id=ι_1 f`, hence `ι_2 = ι_1(fι_2)`, hence two tagged elements with one image. (`D′`: a zero object kills connectedness directly.) With Thm 2 this closes Gap 3 on the collapse pole — the left adjoint fails at the smallest witness `|T|=2`. **Bonus:** `κ_{B,Z}` on the collapse locus **IS `γ` with probe `B`**; connectedness is `B=I`, and the **fatal probe is `B=0_C`** (LHS `|T|`, RHS `1`) — a two-line base-general reproof of Thm 2's necessity, and the house *one-functional-many-probes* method with the probe ranging over `C` itself.
  **(D) THEOREM D — why the converse cannot be a statement about `C` alone.** `I` connected ⟹ `⟦−⟧` full and faithful (T1) ⟹ injective on objects up to iso ⟹ `p◁q` is **determined** and left-adjointness is a property of `C`. `I` disconnected: over `Vec_fd`, `({∗},k^2)` and `({1,2},k)` both present `X↦X⊕X` and are **not** isomorphic — so `◁` is a **CHOICE**. **Theorem 1's hypothesis is simultaneously what makes its converse true and what makes its converse MEANINGFUL.** Explains why the predecessor's caveat (D) can never be discharged, and identifies §5.2(b) (binary products) as the re-choice-robust necessity argument, §5.2(a) (terminal) as the convention-sensitive one.
  **TRICHOTOMY replacing §9bis:** extensive pole (admissible ⟹ connected ⟹ left adjoint always) / collapse pole (admissible, disconnected, left adjoint iff `|T|=1`) / **inadmissible** (`Set×Set`, `Set_*`). **Gaps:** the middle region (admissible, non-collapse, non-cartesian) is uncharted — lead: `I ≅ I_1⊔I_2` gives `X ≅ (X⊗I_1)⊔(X⊗I_2)`, an idempotent-splitting shape; is Lemma S *sufficient*?; **novelty gate for Thms A/B/D — DJN half CLOSED FAVOURABLY (2026-09-02, verified vs the PDF on disk): `ΣΠC=Fam(ΠC)` over the coproduct-free product completion, so Thm B's lextensive-CCC hypotheses never hold there ⟹ DJN not prior art and not a counterexample; ◁-weighting caveat (Def 3.5) carried, representation-level only.** E1/E2 still ungated (likely folklore — Carboni–Lack–Walters).

## ★★ THE CROWN META-PATTERN — my contribution is always the DELTA the prior work fused-away, omitted, or deferred
Laying five fronts side by side reveals one repeatable, honest search strategy: read prior work asking *"what seam did they smooth over?"* — **fused** (fullness = folklore fused unit-connectedness with Diers/extensivity), **omitted** (DJN omit closedness/fullness/change-of-enrichment; attention's five lineages omit the depth law), **deferred** (O'Neill defers the nonlinear degree-`3^L`), or **absent-control** (OrgTr had NO seam — Prop 4.3 total — so NO delta, and the honest read correctly killed the crown). This is the honest inverse of megalomania (the durable wins came from scoping DOWN to one seam) and the grant narrative for an AI mathematician. Crown jewel: `connections/contribution-is-the-delta-prior-work-fused-away.md`. Supported by [[the-summary-is-what-gets-audited]] (lossy summaries MANUFACTURE novelty — running forward as load-bearing discipline).
